00011 tāṃ bhavānīṃ bhavānītakleśanāśaviśāradām |
00012 śāradāṃ śāradāṃ bhodasitaṃsihāsanāṃ numaḥ ||
00021 anapekṣitaguruvacanā sarvāngranthīnvibhedayati samyak |
00022 prakaṭayati pararahasyaṃ vimarśaśaktirnijā jayati ||
00031 dikkālādyanavacchinnānantacinmātramūrtaye |
00032 svānubhūtyekamānāya namaḥ śāntāya tejase ||
00041 jagatsisṛkṣāpralayakriyāvidhau
00042 prayatnamunmeṣanimeṣavibhramam |
00043 vadanti yasyekṣaṇalolapakṣmaṇāṃ
00044 parāya tasmai parameṣṭhine namaḥ ||
00051 namastribhuvanotpattisthitisaṃhārahetave |
00052 viṣṇavepārasaṃsārapārottaraṇasetave ||
00061 surāsuraśiroratnakāntivicchuritāṅghraye |
00062 namastribhuvaneśāya haraye siṃharūpiṇe ||
00071 namastasmai varāhāya helayoddharate mahīm |
00072 khuramadhyagato yasya meruḥ khurakhurāyate ||
00081 namastuṅgaśiraścumbicandracāmaracārave |
00082 trailokyanagarārambhamūlastambhāya śaṃbhave ||
00091 abhipretārthasiddhyarthaṃ pūjito yaḥ surairapi |
00092 sarvavighnacchide tasmai gaṇādhipataye namaḥ ||
00101 nitambālasagāminyaḥ pīnonnatapayodharāḥ |
00102 manmathāya namastasmai yasyāyatanamaṅganāḥ ||
00111 anantamāmadheyāya sarvākaravidhāyine |
00112 samastamantravācyāya viśvaikapataye namaḥ ||
00121 oṃ namaḥ paramārthaikarūpāya paramātmane |
00122 svecchāvabhāsitāsatyabhedabhinnāya śaṃbhave ||
00131 kulaśailadalaṃ pūrṇasuvarṇagirikarṇikam |
00132 namodhitiṣṭhatenantanālaṃ kamalaviṣṭaram ||
00141 karṇikādiṣviva svarṇamarṇavādiṣvivodakam |
00142 bhediṣvabhedi yattasmai parasmai mahase namaḥ ||
00151 namo vāṅmanasātītamahimne parameṣṭhine |
00152 triguṇāṣṭaguṇānantaguṇannirguṇamūrtaye ||
00161 namaḥ śivāya niḥśeṣakleśapraśamaśāline |
00162 triguṇagranthidurbhedabhavabandhavibhedine ||
00171 samastalakṣaṇāyoga eva yasyopalakṣaṇam |
00172 tasmai namostu devāya kasmaicidapi śaṃbhave ||
00181 saṃsāraikanimittāya saṃsāraikavirodhine |
00182 namaḥ saṃsārarūpāya niḥsaṃsārāya śaṃbhave ||
00191 yathā tathāpi yaḥ pūjyo yatra tatrāpi yorcitaḥ |
00192 yopi vā sopi vā yosau devastasmai namostu te ||
00201 sadasattvena bhāvānāṃ yuktā yā dvitayī sthitiḥ |
00202 tāmullaṅghya tṛtīyasmai namaścitrāya śaṃbhave ||
00211 namaḥ svatantracicchaktimudritasvavibhūtaye |
00212 avyaktavyaktarūpāya kasmaicinmantramūrtaye ||
00221 āsannāya sudūrāya guptāya prakaṭātmane |
00222 sulabhāyātidurgāya namaścitrāya śaṃbhave ||
00231 carācarajagatsphārasphurattāmātradharmiṇe |
00232 durvijñeyarahasyāya yuktairapyātmane namaḥ ||
00241 viṣṇurvā tripurāntako bhavatu vā brahmā surendrothavā
00242 bhānurvā śaśalakṣaṇotha bhagavānbuddhotha siddhothavā |
00243 rāgadveṣaviṣārtimoharahitaḥ sattvānukampodyato
00244 yaḥ sarvaiḥ saha saṃskṛto guṇaguṇaistasmai namaḥ sarvadā ||
00251 ślokoyaṃ svāmidattasya tatsmṛtyai kāvyalakṣitaḥ |
00252 yokarotkavināmāṅkaṃ cakrapāṇiyayābhidham ||
00261 bhavabījāṅkurajaladā rāgādyāḥ kṣayamupāgatā yasya |
00262 brahmā vā viṣṇurvā haro jino vā namastasmai ||
00271 kastūrītilakaṃ lalāṭaphalake vakṣaḥsthale kaustubhaṃ
00272 nāsāgre navamauktikaṃ karatale veṇuṃ kare kaṅkaṇam |
00273 sarvāṅge haricandanaṃ suvimalaṃ kaṇṭhe ca muktāvalīṃ
00274 bibhratstrīpariveṣṭito vijayate gopālacūḍāmaṇiḥ ||
00281 aviratāmbujasaṃgatisaṃgaladbahalakesarasaṃvaliteva vaḥ |
00282 lalitavastuvidhānasukhollasattanuruhā tanurātmabhuvovatāt ||
00291 lakṣmīkapolasaṃkrāntakāntapattralatojjvalāḥ |
00292 dordrumāḥ pāntu vaḥ śaurerghanacchāyā mahāphalāḥ ||
00301 pātu vo medinīdolā balendudyutitaskarī |
00302 daṃṣṭrā mahāvarāhasya pātalagṛhadīpakā ||
00311 madamayamadamayaduragaṃ yamunāmavatīrya vīryaśālī yaḥ |
00312 mama ratimamaratiraskṛtiśamanapuraḥ sa kriyātkṛṣṇaḥ ||
00321 sa pātu vo yasya hatāvaśeṣās
00322 tattulyavarṇāñjanarañjiteṣu |
00323 vālaṇyayukteṣvapi vitrasanti
00324 daityāḥ svakāntānayanotpaleṣu ||
00331 caṇḍacāṇūradordaṇḍamaṇḍalīkhaṇḍamaṇḍitam |
00332 avyādvo bālaveṣasya viṣṇorgopatanorvapuḥ ||
00341 govardhanoddharaṇahṛṣṭasamastagopa
00342 nānāstutiśravaṇalajjitamānasasya |
00343 smṛtvā varāhavapurindukalāprakāśa
00344 daṃṣṭroddhṛtakṣiti hareravatu smitaṃ vaḥ ||
00351 manthakṣmādharaghūrṇitārṇavapayaḥ pūrāntarālollasal
00352 lakṣmīkandalakomalāṅgadalanaprādurbhavatsaṃbhramāḥ |
00353 harṣotkaṇṭakitatvaco madhuripordevāsurākarṣaṇa
00354 vyāpāroparamāya pāntu jagatīmābaddhavīpsā giraḥ ||
00361 pṛṣṭhabhrāmyadamandamandaragirigrāvāgrakaṇḍūyanān
00362 nidrāloḥ kamaṭhākṛterbhagavataḥ śvāsānilāḥ pāntu vaḥ |
00363 yatsaṃskārakalānuvartanavaśādvelānibhenāmbhaso
00364 yātāyātamatandritaṃ jalanidhernādyāpi viśrāmyati ||
00371 kiṃcitkuñcitalocanasya pibataḥ paryāptamekaṃ stenaṃ
00372 sadyaḥprasrutadughavindumaparaṃ hastena saṃmārjataḥ |
00373 mātraikāṅgulilālitasya cibuke smerānanasyānanā
00374 cchaureḥ kṣīrakaṇāvalīva patitā dantadyutiḥ pātu vaḥ ||
00381 kālindīpulinodareṣu musalī yāvadgataḥ krīḍituṃ
00382 tāvatkarburikāpayaḥ piba hare vardhiṣyate te śikhā |
00383 itthaṃ bālatayā pratāraṇaparāḥ śrutvā yaśodāgiraḥ
00384 pāyādvaḥ svaśikhāṃ spṛśanpramuditaḥ kṣīrerdhapīte hariḥ ||
00391 ānandena yaśodayā samadanaṃ gopāṅganābhiściraṃ
00392 sāśaṅkaṃ balavidviṣā sakusumaṃ siddhaiḥ pṛthivyākulam |
00393 serṣyaṃ gopakumārakaiḥ sakaruṇaṃ pauraiḥ suraiḥ sasmitaṃ
00394 yo dṛṣṭaḥ sa punātu vo madhuripuḥ protkṣipragovardhanaḥ ||
00401 kṛṣṇenāmba gatena rantumadhunā mṛdbhakṣitā svecchayā
00402 satyaṃ kṛṣṇa ka evamāha musalī mithyāmba paśyānanam |
00403 vyādehīti vikāsitetha vadane mātā samastaṃ jagad
00404 dṛṣṭvā yasya jagāma vismayavaśaṃ pāyātsa vaḥ keśavaḥ ||
00411 kiṃ yuktaṃ bata māmananyamanasaṃ vakṣaḥsthalasthāyinīṃ
00412 bhaktāmapyavadhūya kartumadhunā kāntāsahasraṃ tava |
00413 ityuktvā phaṇabhṛtphaṇāmaṇigatāṃ svāmeva matvā tanuṃ
00414 nidrāchedakaraṃ hareravatu vo lakṣmyā vilakṣasmitam ||
00421 svaprāsāditadarśanāmanunayanprāneśvarīmādarā
00422 daṃsesminpatitairapāṅgavalitairyadbodhitopyaśrubhiḥ |
00423 pratyāyyastvamato mayā nanu hare koyaṃ kramavyatyayaḥ
00424 pātu tvāṃ vrajayoṣitetyabhihitaṃ lajjākarśārṅgiṇaḥ ||
00431 bhaktiprahvavilokanapraṇayiṇī nīlotpalaspardhinī
00432 dhyānālambanatāṃ samādhiniratairnīte hitaprāptaye |
00433 lāvaṇyasya mahānidhī rasikatāṃ lakṣmīdṛśostanvatī
00434 yuṣmākaṃ kurutāṃ bhavārtiharaṇaṃ netre tanurvā hareḥ ||
00441 yena dhvastamanobhavena balijitkāyaḥ purāstrīkṛto
00442 yaścodvṛttabhujaṃgarahāravalayo gaṅgāṃ ca yodhārayat |
00443 yasyāhuḥ śaśimacchiro hara iti stutyaṃ ca nāmāmarāḥ
00444 pāpātsa svayamandhakakṣayakarastvāṃ sarvadomādhavaḥ ||
00451 kiṃcinnirmucyamāne gagana iva mukhe nāṭyanidrāpayodair
00452 nyakkurvāṇe svabhāsā phaṇipatiśirasāṃ ratnadīpāṃśujālam |
00453 pāyāstāṃ vo muroreḥ śaśitapanamaye locane yadvibhāsā
00454 lakṣmyā kastasthamardhaṃ vikasati kamalasyārdhamabhyeti nidrām ||
00461 mallaiḥ śailendrakalpaḥ śiśurakhilajanaiḥ puṣpacāpoṅganābhir
00462 gopaistu prākṛtātmā divi kuliśabhṛtā viśvakāyoprameyaḥ |
00463 kruddhaḥ kaṃsena kālo bhayacakitadṛśā yogibhirdhyeyamūrtir
00464 dṛṣṭo raṅgāvatāre hariramarajanānandakṛtpātu yuṣmān ||
00471 bhindannarātihṛdayāni hareḥ punātu
00472 niḥśvāsavātamukharīkṛtakoṭaro vaḥ |
00473 saṃkrāntakukṣikuharāspadasaptasindhu
00474 saṃghaṭṭaghorataraghoṣa ivāśu śaṅkhaḥ ||
00481 pāyātsa vaḥ kumudakundamṛṇālagauraḥ
00482 śaṅkho hareḥ karatalāmbarapūrṇacandraḥ |
00483 nādena yasya suraśatruvilāsinīnāṃ
00484 kāñcyo bhavanti śithilā jaghanasthalīṣu ||
00491 dṛṣṭasya yasya hariṇā raṇamūrdhni mūrtir
00492 udbhūtaduḥsahamahaḥprasarā samantāt |
00493 tallocanasthitaravipratibimbagarbhe
00494 vābhāti cakramaricakranudestu tadvaḥ ||
00501 udvṛttadaityapṛtanāpatikaṇṭhapīṭha
00502 cchedocchaladbahalaśoṇitaśoṇadhāram |
00503 cakraṃ kriyādabhimatāni harerudāra
00504 digdāhadāruṇanabhaḥśriyamudvahadvaḥ ||
00511 yasyodyadbāṇavāhudrumagahanavanacchedagoṣṭhīkuṭhāraṃ
00512 cakraṃ niṣkrāntatīvrānalabahalakaṇākīrṇadhāraṃ vicintya |
00513 jātagrāsāvasāyo divasakṛtisalanmāṃsalāṃśupravāhe
00514 muhyatyadyāpi rāhuḥ sa dahatu duritānyāśu daityāntako vaḥ ||
00521 daityāsthipañjaravidāraṇalabdharandhra
00522 raktāmbunirjarasariddhanajātapaṅkāḥ |
00523 bālendukoṭikuṭilāḥ śukacañcubhāso
00524 rakṣantu siṃhavapuṣo nakharā harervaḥ ||
00531 ādityāḥ kiṃ daśaite pralayabhayakṛtaḥ svīkṛtākāśadeśāḥ
00532 kiṃ volkāmaṇḍalāni tribhuvanadahanāyodyatānīti bhītaiḥ |
00533 pāyāsurnārasiṃhaṃ vapuramaragaṇairbibhrataḥ śārṅgapāṇer
00534 dṛṣṭvā dṛptāsurorastaladaraṇagaladraktaraktā nakhā vaḥ ||
00541 merūrukesaramudāradigantapattram
00542 āmūlalambicalaśeṣaśarīranālam |
00543 yenoddhṛtaṃ kuvalayaṃ salilātsalīlam
00544 uttaṃsakārthamiva pātu sa vo varāhaḥ ||
00551 na mṛdnīyānmṛdvī kathamiva mahī potranikaṣair
00552 mukhāgnijvālābhiḥ kanakagirirīyānna vilayam |
00553 na śuṣyeyuḥ śvāsaiḥ salilanidhayaḥ sapta ca kathaṃ
00554 varāho vaḥ pāyāditi vipulacintāparikaraḥ ||
00561 svāmī sanbhuvanatrayasya vikṛtiṃ nītosi kiṃ yācñayā
00562 yadvā viśvasṛjā tvayaiva na kṛtaṃ taddīyatāṃ te kutaḥ |
00563 dānaṃ śreṣṭhatamāya tubhyamatulaṃ bandhāya no muktaye
00564 vijñapto balinā niruttaratayā hrīto hariḥ pātu vaḥ ||
00571 līne śrotraikadeśe nabhasi nayanayostejasi kvāpi yāte
00572 śvāsagrāsopayukte maruti jalanidhau pāyurandhrārdhapīte |
00573 potraprāntaikaromāntaravivaragatāṃ mṛgyataḥ śārṅgapāṇeḥ
00574 kroḍākārasya pṛthvīmakalitaviṣayaṃ vaibhavaṃ vaḥ punātu ||
00581 kvedānīṃ darpitāste ghanamadamadirāmodino digdhipendrā
00582 he mero mandarāndre malaya himagire sādhu vaḥ kṣmādharatvam |
00583 śeṣa ślāghyosi dīrghaiḥ pṛthubhuvanabharoccaṇḍaśauṇḍaiḥ śirobhiḥ
00584 śaṃsansotprāsamuccairiti dharaṇibhṛtaḥ pātu yuṣmānvarāhaḥ ||
00591 āvyādvo vāmano yasya kaustubhapratibimbitā |
00592 kautukālokinī jātā jāṭharīva jagattrayī ||
00601 ekasthaṃ jīviteśe tvayi sakalajagatsāramālokayāmaḥ
00602 śyāme cakṣustavāsminvapuṣi niviśate nālpapuṇyasya puṃsaḥ |
00603 kasyānyatrāmṛtesminratirativipulā dṛṣṭirevāmṛtaṃ te
00604 daityairityucyamāno munibhirapi hariḥ straiṇarūpovatādvaḥ ||
00611 bhrāmyanmahāgirinigharṣaṇalabdhapṛṣṭha
00612 kaṇḍūyanakṣaṇasukhāyitagāḍhanidraḥ |
00613 suṣvāpa dīrghataraghargharaghoraghoṣaḥ
00614 śvāsābhibhūtajaladhiḥ kamaṭhaḥ sa vovyātu ||
00621 sa dhūrjaṭijaṭājūṭo jāyatāṃ vijayāya vaḥ |
00622 yasyaikapalitabhrāntiṃ karotpadyāpi jāhnavī ||
00631 sa pātu vo yasya jaṭākalāpe
00632 sthitaḥ śaśāṅkaḥ sphuṭahāragauraḥ |
00633 nīlotpalānāmiva nālapuñje
00634 nidrāyamāṇaḥ śaradīva haṃsaḥ ||
00641 diśyātsa śītakiraṇābharaṇaḥ śivaṃ vo
00642 yasyottamāṅgabhuvi visphuradūrmipakṣā |
00643 haṃsīva nirmalaśaśāṅkakalāmṛṇāla
00644 kandārthinī surasarinnabhasaḥ papāta ||
00651 śreyāṃsi vo diśatu yasya sitābhraśubhrā
00652 vibhrājate surasaridvaramaulimālā |
00653 ūrdhvekṣaṇajvalanatāpavilīyamāna
00654 candrāmṛtapravitatāmṛtavāhinīva ||
00661 cyutāmindorlekhāṃ ratikalahabhagnaṃ ca valayaṃ
00662 śanairekīkṛtya prahasitamukhī śailatanayā |
00663 avocadyaṃ paśyotyavatu sa śivaḥ sā ca girijā
00664 sa ca krīḍācandro daśanakiraṇāpūritatanuḥ ||
00671 eṣā te hara kā sugātri katamā mūrdhni sthitā kiṃ jaṭā
00672 haṃsaḥ kiṃ bhajate jaṭāṃ nahi śaśī candro jalaṃ sevate |
00673 mugdho bhūtiriyaṃ kutotra salilaṃ bhūtistaraṅgāyate
00674 evaṃ yo vinigūhate tripathagāṃ pāyātsa vaḥ śaṃkaraḥ ||
00681 āśleṣādharavimbacumbanasukhālāpasmitānyāsatāṃ
00682 dūre tāvadidaṃ mitho na sulabhaṃ jātaṃ mukhālokanam |
00683 itthaṃ vyarthakṛtaikadehaghaṭanopanyāsayorāvayoḥ
00684 keyaṃ premaviḍambanetyavatu vaḥ smerordhanārīśvaraḥ ||
00691 mātarjīva kimetadañjalipuṭe tātena gopāyyate
00692 vatsa svādu phalaṃ prayacchati na me gatvā gṛhāṇa svayam |
00693 mātraivaṃ prahite gṛhe vighaṭayatyākṛṣya saṃdhyāñjaliṃ
00694 śaṃbhorbhinnasamādhiruddharabhaso hāsodgamaḥ pātu vaḥ ||
00701 ekaṃ cantacchadasya sphurati japavaśādardhamanyatprakopād
00702 ekaḥ pāṇiḥ praṇantuṃ śirasi kṛtapadaḥ kṣeptumanyastameva |
00703 ekaṃ dhyānānnimīlatyaparamaviṣahaṃ vīkṣituṃ cakṣuritthaṃ
00704 tulyānicchāpi vāmā tanuravatu sa vo yasya saṃdhyāvasāne ||
00711 śailarājatanayāstanayagmavyāpṛtāsyayugalasya gṛhasya |
00712 śeṣavakrakamalāni malaṃ vo dugdhapānavidhurāṇi harantu ||
00721 karajālamapūrvaceṣṭitaṃ vastadabhīṣṭapradamastu tigmabhāsaaḥ |
00722 kriyate bhavabandhanādvimuktiḥ praṇatānāmupasevitena yena ||
00731 yuṣmākamambaramaṇe prathame mayūkhās
00732 te maṅgalaṃ vidadhatūdayarāgabhājaḥ |
00733 kurvanti ye divasajanmamahotsaveṣu
00734 sindūrapāṭalamukhīriva dikpuraṃdhrīḥ ||
00741 ābāhūdgatamaṇḍalāgrarucayaḥ saṃnaddhavakṣaḥsthalāḥ
00742 soṣmāṇo vraṇino vipakṣahṛdaypronmāthinaḥ karkaśāḥ |
00743 utsṛṣṭāmbaradṛṣṭavigrahabharā yasya smarāgresarā
00744 yodhā vāravadhūstanāśca na dadhuḥ kṣobhaṃ sa vovyājjinaḥ ||
00751 kiṃ syādbhāsvānna bhānoramṛtaghanarasasyandinaḥ santi pādāḥ
00752 kiṃ vā rākāśaśāṅko nahi tuhinaruciḥ kutracinniṣkalaṅkaḥ |
00753 sākṣāccintāmaṇiḥ kiṃ vipulaphalamaṇeḥ saukumāryaṃ kutastyaṃ
00754 saṃdehānmugdhadhībhiḥ prathamamiti muneḥ pātu dṛṣṭaṃ vapurvaḥ ||
00761 ciramāviṣkṛtaprītibhītayaḥ pāntu vo dviṣām |
00762 valayajyāravonmiśrāścaṇḍyāḥ kodaṇḍakṛṣṭayaḥ ||
00771 diśyānmahāsuraśiraḥsarasīpsitāni
00772 preṅkhannakhāvalimayūkhamṛṇālanālam |
00773 caṇḍyāścalaccaṭulanūpuracañcarīka
00774 jhāṃkārahāri caraṇāmburuhadvayaṃ vaḥ ||
00781 savrīḍā dayitānane sakaruṇā mātaṅgacarmāmbare
00782 satrāsā bhujage savismayarasā candremṛtasyandini |
00783 serṣyā jahnusutāvalokanavidhau dīnā kapālodare
00784 pārvatyā navasaṃgamapraṇayinī dṛṣṭiḥ śivāyastu vaḥ ||
00791 uttiṣṭhantyā ratānte bharamuragapatau pāṇinaikena kṛtvā
00792 dhṛtvā cānyena vāso vigalitakabarībhāramaṃsaṃ vahantyāḥ |
00793 bhūyastatkālakāntidviguṇitasurataprītinā śauriṇā vaḥ
00794 śayyāmāliṅgya nītaṃ vapuralasalasadbāhu lakṣmyāḥ punātu ||
00801 pāyādgajendravadanaḥ sa imāṃ trilokīṃ
00802 yasyodgatena gagane mahatā kareṇa |
00803 mūlāvalagnasitadantabisāṅkureṇa
00804 nālāyitaṃ tapanabimbasaroruhasya ||
00811 sānandaṃ nandihastāhatamurajaravāhvatakaumārabarhi
00812 trāsānnāsāgrarandhraṃ viśati phaṇipatau bhogasaṃkocabhāji |
00813 gaṇḍoḍḍīnālimālāmukharitakakubhastāṇḍave śūlapāṇer
00814 vaināyakyaściraṃ vo vadanavidhutayaḥ pāntu sītkāravatyaḥ ||
00821 dhanurmālā maurvī kvaṇadalikulaṃ lakṣyamabalā
00822 mano bhedyaṃ śabdaprabhṛtaya ime pañca viśikhāḥ |
00823 iyāñjetuṃ yasya tribhuvanamadehasya vibhavaḥ
00824 sa kāmaḥ kāmānvo diśatu dayitāpāṅgavasatiḥ ||
00831 brahmā dakṣaḥ kubero yamavaruṇamarudvahnicandrendrarudrāḥ
00832 śailā nadyaḥ samudrā grahagaṇamanujā daityagandharvanāgāḥ |
00833 dvīḥā nakṣatratārāravivasumunayo vyoma bhūraścinau ca
00834 saṃlīnā yasya sarve vapuṣi sa bhagavāpātu vo viśvarūpaḥ ||
00841 mugdhe muñca viṣādamatra balajitkampo gurustyajyatāṃ
00842 sadbhāvaṃ bhaja puṇḍarīkanayane mānyānimānmānaya |
00843 lakṣmīṃ bodhayataḥ svayaṃvaravidhau dhanvantarervākchalā
00844 danyatra pratiṣedhamātmani vidhiṃ śṛṇvanhariḥ pātu vaḥ ||
00851 diśyātsukhaṃ naraharirbhuvanaikavīro
00852 yasyāhave ditisutoddalanodyatasya |
00853 krodhoddhataṃ mukhamavekṣitumakṣamatvaṃ
00854 jānebhavannijanakheṣvapi yannatāste ||
00861 svasti svāgatamarthyahaṃ vada vibho kiṃ dīyatāṃ medinī
00862 kā mātrā mama vikramatrayapadaṃ dattaṃ gṛhītāṃ mayā |
00863 mā dehītyuśanāḥ kuto harirayaṃ pātraṃ kimasmātparaṃ
00864 yo hītthaṃ balinārcito makhamukhe pāyātsa vo vāmanaḥ ||
00871 cakra brūhi vibho gade jaya hare kambo samājñāpaya
00872 bho bho nandaka jīva pannagariyo kiṃ nātha bhinno mayā |
00873 ko daityaḥ katamo hiraṇyakaśipuḥ satyaṃ bhavadbhyaḥ śape
00874 kenāstreṇa nakhairiti pravadato viṣṇormukhaṃ pātu vaḥ ||
00881 cintācakriṇi hanta cakriṇi bhiyā kubjāsanebjāsane
00882 naśyaddhāmani tigmadhāmani dhṛtāśaṅke śaśāṅke bhṛśam |
00883 bhraśyaccetasi ca pracetasi śucā tānte kṛtānte ca yo
00884 vyagrobhūtkaṭukālakūṭakavalīkārāya pāyātsa vaḥ ||
00891 nityaṃ narāvṛti nijānubhavaikamānam
00892 ānandadhāma jagadaṅkurabījamekam |
00893 digdeśakālakalanādisamastahasta
00894 mardāsahaṃ diśatu śarma mahanmaho vaḥ ||
00901 vyomnīva nīradabharaḥ sarasīva vīci
00902 vyūhaḥ sahasramahasīva sudhāṃśudhām |
00903 yasminnidaṃ jagadudeti ca līyate ca
00904 tacchāmbhavaṃ bhavatu vaibhavamṛrddhaye vaḥ ||
00911 lokatrayasthitilayodayakelikāraḥ
00912 kāryeṇa yo hariharadruhiṇatvameti |
00913 devaḥ sa viśvajanavāṅmanasātivṛtti
00914 śaktiḥ śivaṃ diśatu śaśvadanaśvaraṃ vaḥ ||
00921 sarvaḥ kilāyamavaśaḥ puruṣāṇukarma
00922 kāyādikāraṇagaṇo yadanugraheṇa |
00923 viśva prapañcaracanācaturatvameti
00924 sa trāyatāṃ tribhuvanaikamaheśvaro vaḥ ||
00931 yaḥ kandukairiva puraṃdara padmasadma
00932 padmāpatiprabhṛtibhiḥ prabhuraprameyaḥ |
00933 khelatyalaṅghyamahimā sa himādrikanyā
00934 kāntaḥ kṛtāntadalano laghayatvadhaṃ vaḥ ||
00941 muktirhi nāma paramaḥ puruṣārtha ekas
00942 tāmantarāyamavayanti yadantarajñāḥ |
00943 kiṃ bhūyasā bhavatu saiva sudhāmayūkha
00944 lekhāśikhābharaṇabhaktirabhaṅgurā vaḥ ||
00951 śrīkaṇṭhasya sakṛttikārtabharaṇī mūrtiḥ sadārohiṇī
00952 jyeṣṭhā bhadrapadā punarvasuyutā citrā viśākhānvitā |
00953 diśyādakṣatahastamūlaghaṭitāṣāḍhā maghālaṅkṛtā
00954 śreyo vaiśravaṇānvitā bhagavato nakṣatrapālīva vaḥ ||
00961 trātā bhītibhṛtāṃ patiścidacitāṃ kleśaṃ sataṃ śaṃsatāṃ
00962 hantā bhaktimatāṃ satāṃ svasamatāṃ kartāpakartāsatām |
00963 devaḥ sevakabhuktimuktiracanābhūrbhūrbhuvaḥsvastrayī
00964 nirmāṇasthitisaṃhṛtiprakaṭitakrīḍo mṛḍaḥ pātu vaḥ ||
00971 rājā rājārcitāṅghreranupacitakalo yasya cūḍāmaṇitvaṃ
00972 nāgā gāgātmajārdhaṃ na bhasitadhavalaṃ yadvapurbhūṣayanti |
00973 mā rāmārāgiṇī bhūnmatiriti yamināṃ yena vodāhi māraḥ
00974 sa ptāḥ saptāśvanunnāruṇakiraṇanibhāḥ pātu bibhrattrinetraḥ ||
00981 rādhāmohanamandiraṃ jagamiṣoścandrāvalīmandirād
00982 rādho kṣemamiti priyasya vacanaṃ śrutvāha candrāvalī |
00983 kṣemaṃ kaṃsa tataḥ priyaḥ prakupitaḥ kaṃsaḥ kva dṛṣṭastvayā
00984 rādhā kveti tayoḥ prisannamanasorhāsodgamaḥ pātu vaḥ ||
00991 ākṛṣṭe yudhi kārmuke samavadadvāmaḥ karo dakṣiṇaṃ
00992 re re dakṣiṇahasta bhojanamahādānādi te kurvataḥ |
00993 paścādgantumayuktamityatha punaḥ sopyabravīdadravaṃ
00994 praṣṭuṃ rāghavamāśu rāvaṇaśirovṛndāni bhindāni kim ||
01001 dṛṣṭaḥ kvāpi sa keśavo vrajavadhūmādāya kāṃcidgataḥ
01002 sarvā eva hi vañcitāḥ khula vayaṃ sonveṣaṇīyo yadi |
01003 dve dve gacchata ityudīrya sahasā rādhāṃ gṛhītvā kare
01004 gopīveṣadharo nikuñjabhavanaṃ prāpto hariḥ pātu vaḥ ||
01011 mātastarṇakarakṣaṇāya yamunākacchaṃ na gacchāmyahaṃ
01012 kasmādvatsa pinaṣṭi pīvarakucadvandvena gopījanaḥ |
01013 bhrūbhaṅgairvinivāritopi bahuśo jalpanyaśodāgrato
01014 gopībhiḥ karapadmamudritamukhaḥ pāyātsa vaḥ keśavaḥ ||
01021 saṃdhyāṃ yatpraṇipatya lokapurato baddhāñjaliryācase
01022 dhatse yacca nadīṃ vilajja śirasā tannāma soḍhaṃ mayā |
01023 śrīryātāmṛtamanthane yadi hariṃ kasmādviṣaṃ bhakṣitaṃ
01024 mā strīlaṃpaṭa māṃ spṛśeti gadito gauryā haraḥ pātu vaḥ ||
01031 kastvaṃ śūlī mṛgaya bhiṣajaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaḥ priyehaṃ
01032 kekāmekāṃ vada paśupatirnaiva dṛśye viṣāṇe |
01033 mugdhe sthāṇuḥ sa carati kathaṃ jīviteśaḥ śivāyā
01034 gacchāṭavyāmiti hatavacāḥ pātu vaścandracūḍaḥ ||
01041 koyaṃ dvāri hariḥ prayāhyupavanaḥ śākhāmṛgasyātra kiṃ
01042 kṛṣṇohaṃ dayite bibhemi sutarāṃ kṛṣṇādahaṃ vānarāt |
01043 mugdhehaṃ madhusūdanaḥ piba latāṃ tāmeva tanmīvale
01044 itthaṃ nirvacanīkṛto dayitayā hrīto hariḥ pātu vaḥ ||
01051 nirlajja hare kimidaṃ pramadānugataḥ sadā paribhramasi |
01052 mugdhe tvatsaṃparkātpramado bhavatīti kiṃ citram ||
01061 śaṭha varṇayāmi bhavato nārīṇāmuparibhūyasī prītiḥ |
01062 pralapasi kimasaṃbaddhaṃ kasyāriṣu vidyate prema ||
01071 vyāmohayasi kimevaṃ rāmāsaktiṃ bravīmi bhavadīyām |
01072 jyeṣṭhe bhrātari rāme na kriyatāṃ kathamivāsaktiḥ ||
01081 kiṃ māmevaṃ bhramayasi śocāmi vyasanameva bhavadīyam |
01082 niṣkāraṇakupitāyāṃ tvayi kathaya kimalpakaṃ vyasanam ||
01091 vakravacanairamībhirgopavadhūmiti naruttarīkṛtya |
01092 maṇḍalitagurupayodharamupagūḍhaṃ pātu vaḥ śaureḥ ||
01101 ayi saṃprasīda pārvati śivopi tava pādayornipatitoham |
01102 śiva iti kathaṃ hi jalpasi sarudhiragajacarmasaṃvītaḥ ||
01111 śiva iti yadi tava gadite dviguṇo roṣo bhavāmyahaṃ sthāṇuḥ |
01112 sthānurasi satyametaccetasi bhavato na kiṃcidapi ||
01121 tyaja ruṣamavehi mānini māmīśvaramarcitaḥ tribhuvanasya |
01122 tryambaka yadīśvarastvaṃ nagnaḥ kiṃ dhūlidhūsaritaḥ ||
01131 saṃprati kimatra vakṣyasi paśupatireṣosmi pāṇḍurakapole |
01132 paśupatireva na gaṇayasi yuktāyuktāni yasmāttvam ||
01141 mugdhe bhramasi kimevaṃ satyamimaṃ māṃ bhavaṃ vijānīhi |
01142 satyaṃ bhavosi śaṭha he yenātivicitrarūposi ||
01151 paṇḍitabādastava yadi lokehaṃ tryambako vidita eṣaḥ |
01152 ambā hyekāpi na te prajalpasi tvaṃ kutastisraḥ ||
01161 vādo mahānihaiva hi tathā vijānīhyanaṅgadahanaṃ mām |
01162 dagdhamidamaṅgamaṅgaṃ tvayā mamaivedṛśaiścaritaiḥ ||
01171 saṃdhyāpraṇāmadoṣādyonunayati taṃ vijitya pārvatyā |
01172 āliṅgitaśca sarabhasamurasā vai haratu duritaṃ vaḥ ||
01181 bhava niḥsnehastvaṃ me na bhavāmyevaṃ yathā tvayā gaditam |
01182 niḥsnehatābhilāṣastava devi kutaḥ samutpannaḥ ||
01191 kusṛtibhiralametābhiḥ kimarthamuparisthitā nadīyaṃ te |
01192 kā narakapālamālā mamoparisthā gṛhāṇaitāḥ ||
01201 janamanurāgiṇamevaṃ saṃtāpayasi vyalīkakaraṇena |
01202 tava narakapālapaṅktibhiravaśyamevopari stheyam ||
01211 kiṃ kupitosi tyaja ruṣamapanayatāṃ vigrahaṃ mayā hi bhavān |
01212 saha vigraho bhavatyā na jātu vighāṭiṣyatesmākam ||
01221 gaṅgāvigrahakaluṣāmiti śivavacanairniruttarāṃ gaurīm |
01222 parihāsya yonuninye sa karotu śivaḥ śivaṃ bhavatām ||
01231 vijaye kuśalastryakṣo na krīḍitumahamanena saha śaktā |
01232 vijaye kuśalosmi na tu tryakṣokṣadvayamidaṃ pāṇau ||
01241 kiṃ me durodareṇa prayātu yadi gaṇapatirna tebhimataḥ |
01242 kaḥ pradveṣṭi vināyakamahilokaḥ kiṃ na jānāsi ||
01251 vasurahitena krīḍā bhavatā saha kīdṛśī na jihreṣi |
01252 kiṃ vasubhinnamatomūnsurāsurāneva paśya puraḥ ||
01261 candragrahaṇena vinā nāsmi rame kiṃ pravartayasyevam |
01262 devyai yadi rucitamidaṃ nandinnāhvayatāṃ rāhuḥ ||
01271 hā rāhau nikaṭasthe sitadaṃṣṭre bhayakṛti ratiḥ kasya |
01272 yadi necchasi tattyaktaḥ saṃpratyevaiṣa hārāhiḥ ||
01281 āropayasi mudhā kiṃ nāhamabhijñā tvadaṅkasya |
01282 divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ sthitvaivaṃ yuktamabhidhātum ||
01291 itthaṃ paśupatipeśalapāśakalīlāprayuktavakrokteḥ |
01292 harṣavaśataralatārakamānanamavyādbhavānyā vaḥ ||
01301 aṅgulyā kaḥ kavāṭe praharati kuṭilo mādhavaḥ kiṃ vasanto
01302 no cakrī kiṃ kulālo nahi dharaṇidharaḥ kiṃ praṇīndro dvijihvaḥ |
01303 mugdhe ghorāhimāthī kimuta khagapatirno hariḥ kiṃ kapīndra
01304 itthaṃ lakṣmyā kṛtosau pratihatavacanaḥ pātulakṣmīdhavo vaḥ ||
01311 khedaḥ kiṃ khalu dayite na vetsi ravimaṇḍalaṃ jagadviditam |
01312 na krodhaḥ kartavyo jalacaramūrdhvaṃ na jātu paśyāmaḥ ||
01321 kopastyaktuṃ yogyo yasya pipāsā na saṃbhavati |
01322 saṃtyaja mānini mānaṃ kiṃ mānenādhunā mamānena ||
01331 kiṃ tena kila kāvyena mṛdyamānasya yasya tāḥ |
01332 udadheriva nāyānti rasāmṛtaparamparāḥ ||
01341 kiṃ kavestasya kāvyena kiṃ kāṇḍena dhanuṣmataḥ |
01342 parasya hṛdaye lagnaṃ na ghūrṇayati yacchiraḥ ||
01351 apragalbhapadanyāsā jananīrāgahetavaḥ |
01352 santyeke bahulālāpāḥ kavayo bālakā iva ||
01361 kiṃ tena kāvyamadhunā plāvitā rasanirjharaiḥ |
01362 jaḍātmānopi no yasya bhavantyaṅkuritān tarāḥ ||
01371 navortho jātiragrāmyā śleṣokliṣṭaḥ sphuto rasaḥ |
01372 vikaṭākṣarabandhaśca kṛtsnamekatra duṣkaram ||
01381 mukhamātreṇa kāvyasya karotyahṛdayo janaḥ |
01382 chāyāmachāmapi śyāmāṃ rāhustārāpateriva ||
01391 boddhāromatsaragrastā vibhavaḥ smayadūṣitāḥ |
01392 abodhopahatāścānye jīrnamaṅge subhāṣitam ||
01401 padadvayasya saṃdhānaṃ kartumapratibhāḥ khalāḥ |
01402 tathāpi parakāvyeṣu duṣkareṣvapyasaṃbhramāḥ ||
01411 kva doṣotra mayā labhya iti saṃcintya cetasā |
01412 khalaḥ kāvyeṣu sādhūnāṃ śravaṇāya pravartate ||
01421 upapattibhiramlānā nopadeśaiḥ kadarthitāḥ |
01422 svasaṃvedanasaṃvedyasārāḥ sahṛdayoktayaḥ ||
01431 keṣāṃcidvāci śukavatpareṣāṃ hṛdi mūkavat |
01432 kasyāpyā hṛdayādvaktre valgu valganti sūktayaḥ  ||
01441 bahūni naraśīrṣāṇi lomaśāni bṛhanti ca |
01442 grīvāsu pratibaddhāni kiṃcitteṣu sakarṇakam ||
01451 sādhvīva bhāratī bhāti sūktisadvratacāriṇī |
01452 grāmyārthavastusaṃsparśabahiraṅgā mahākaveḥ ||
01461 te vandyāste mahātmānasteṣāṃ loke sthiraṃ yaśaḥ |
01462 yairnibaddhāni kāvyānī ye vā kāvyeṣu kīrtitāḥ ||
01471 prasannāḥ kāntihāriṇyo nānāśleṣavicakṣaṇāḥ |
01472 bhavanti kasyacitpuṇyairmukhe vāco gṛhe striyaḥ ||
01481 kavīnāṃ mahatāṃ sūktairgūḍhārthāntarasūcibhiḥ |
01482 vidhyamānaśrutermā bhūhurjanasya kathaṃ vyathā ||
01491 yāsyati sajjanahastaṃ ramayiṣyati taṃ bhavecca nirdoṣā |
01492 utpāditayāpi kavistāmyati kathayā duhitreva ||
01501 avasarapaṭhitaṃ sarvaṃ subhāṣitatvaṃ prayātpasūktamapi |
01502 kṣudhi kadaśanamapi nitarāṃ bhoktuḥ saṃpadyate svādu ||
01511 durjanahutāśataptaṃ kāvyasuvarṇaṃ viśuddhimupayāti |
01512 darśayitavyaṃ tasmānmatsarimanasaḥ prayatnena ||
01521 gaṇayanti nāpaśabdaṃ na vṛttabhaṅgaṃ kṣatiṃ na cārthasya |
01522 rasikatvenākulitā veśyāpatayaḥ kukavayaśca ||
01531 vipulahṛdayābhiyogye khidyati kāvye jaḍo na maurkhye sve |
01532 nindati kañcukameva prāyaḥ śuṣkastanā nārī ||
01541 khyātiṃ gamayati sujanaḥ sukavirvidadhāti kevalaṃ kāvyam |
01542 puṣṇāti kamalamambho lakṣmyā tu ravirniyojayati ||
01551 vyākhyātumeva kecitkuśalāḥ śāstraṃ prayoktumalamanye |
01552 upanāmayati karonnaṃ rasāṃstu jihbaiva jānāti ||
01561 jivita iva kaṇṭhagate sūkte duḥkhāsikā kavestāvat |
01562 nayanavikāsavidhāyī sacetanābhyāgamo yāvat ||
01571 pratīyamānaṃ punaranyadeva
01572 vastvasti vāṇīṣu mahākavīnām |
01573 yattatprasiddhāvayavātirikta
01574 mābhāti lāvaṇyamivāṅganāsu ||
01581 kaverabhiprāyamaśabdagocaraṃ
01582 sphurantamārdreṣu padeṣu kevalam |
01583 vadadbhiraṅgaiḥ kṛtaromavikriyair
01584 janasya tūṣṇīṃbhavatoyamañjaliḥ ||
01591 sahṛdayāḥ kavigumphanikāsu ye
01592 katipayāsta ime na viśṛṅkhalāḥ |
01593 rasamayīṣu latāsviva ṣaṭpadā
01594 hṛdayasārajuṣo na mukhaspṛśaḥ ||
01601 khyātā narādhipatayaḥ kavisaṃśrayeṇa
01602 rājāśrayeṇa ca gatāḥ kavayaḥ prasiddhim |
01603 rājñā samosti na kaveḥ paramopakārī
01604 rājño na cāsti kavinā sadṛśaḥ sahāyaḥ ||
01611 cetaḥprasādajananaḥ vibuddhottamānām
01612 ānandi sarvarasayuktamatiprasannam |
01613 kāvyaṃ khalasya na karoti hṛdi pratiṣṭhāṃ
01614 pīyūṣapānamiva vaktravivarti rāhoḥ ||
01621 baddhā yadarpaṇarasena vimardapūrvam
01622 arthānkathaṃ jhaṭiti tānprakṛtānna dadyuḥ |
01623 caurā ivātimṛdavo mahatāṃ kavīnām
01624 arthāntarāṇyapi haṭhādvitaranti śabdāḥ ||
01631 tatkiṃ kāvyamanalpapītamadhuvatkuryānna yaddhṛdgataṃ
01632 mātsaryāvṛtacetasāṃ rasavaśādapyudgatiṃ  lomasu |
01633 kampaṃ mūrdhni kapolayugmamaruṇaṃ bāṣpāvile locane
01634 adhyāropitavastukīrtanaparaṃ vācaḥ karālambanam ||
01641 ye tāvatsvaguṇopabṛṃhitadhiyasteṣāmaraṇyaṃ jagad
01642 yepyete kṛtamatsarāḥ paraguṇaṃ svapnepi necchanti te |
01643 anyeṣāmanurāgiṇāṃ kvacidapi snigdhaṃ mano nirvṛtā
01644 vitthaṃ yāntu tapovanāni mahatāṃ sūktāni manyedhunā ||
01651 yā sādhūniva sādhuvādamukharānmātsaryamūkānapi
01652 proccairno kurute satāṃ matimatāṃ dṛṣṭirna sā vāstavī |
01653 yā yātāḥ śrutigocaraṃ ca sahasā harṣollasatkaṃdharās
01654 tiryañcopi na muktaśaṣpakavalāstāḥ kiṃ kavīnāṃ giraḥ ||
01661 svecchābhaṅgurabhāgyameghataḍitaḥ śakyā na roddhuṃ śriyaḥ
01662 prāṇānāṃ satataṃ prayāṇapaṭahaśraddhā na viśrāmyati |
01663 trāṇaṃ yetra yaśomaye vapuṣi vaḥ kurvanti kāvyāmṛtais
01664 tānārādhyapade vidhatta sukavīnnirgarvamurvīśvarāḥ ||
01671 he rājānastyajata sukavipremabandhe virodhaṃ
01672 śuddhā kīrtiḥ sphurati bhavatāṃ nūnametatprasādāt |
01673 tuṣṭairbaddhaṃ tadalaghu raghusvāminaḥ saccaritraṃ
01674 ruṣṭairnītastribhuvanajayī hāsyamārgaṃ daśāsyaḥ ||
01681 namo namaḥ kāvyarasāya tasmai
01682 niṣiktamantaḥ pṛṣatāpi yasya |
01683 suvarṇatāṃ vaktramupaiti sādhor
01684 durvarṇatāṃ yāti ca durjanasya ||
01691 ajñātapāṇḍityarahasyamudrā
01692 ye kāvyamārge dadhatebhimānam |
01693 te gāruḍīyānanadhītya mantrān
01694 hālāhalāsvādanamārabhante ||
01701 sarasvatīmāturabhūcciraṃ na yaḥ
01702 kavitvapāṇḍityaghanastanaṃdhayaḥ |
01703 kathaṃ sa sarvāṅgamanāptasauṣṭhavo
01704 dināddinaṃ prauḍhiviśeṣamaśnute ||

02061 rasāyanamayī śītā paramānandadāyinī |

02122 khalavāgviśikhaistīkṣṇairbhidyante na manāgyataḥ ||

02161 guṇarāśimahābhāranirbharāpūritāntarāḥ |

02171 svātmanyeva layaṃ yātu tādṛśo guṇināṃ guṇaḥ |

02621 cirāya satsaṃgamaśuddhamānaso

04661 aguṇakaṇo guṇorāśirdvayamiha daivātkhalamukhe patitam |

06411 lāṅgūlacālanamadhaścaraṇāvapātaṃ

06611 puro revā pāre giriratidurārohaśikharaḥ

06661 karabhadayite yattatpītaṃ sudurlabhamekadā

06663 kuru paricitaiḥ pīloḥ pattrairdhṛtiṃ marugocare

06671 karabhadayite yosau pīlustvayā madhulubdhayā

06691 karabha rabhasātkroṣṭuṃ vāñchasyaho śravaṇajvaraḥ

06702 caransvasthoraṇye karabhaśiśukaḥ śokarahitaḥ |

06722 śākhāgraṃ yadakhādi cāru karabhīvaktrārpitaṃ premataḥ |

07852 yathāphalarddhisvārohā hā mātaḥ kvāgamandrumāḥ ||
07861 sādhveva tadvidhāvasya vedhāḥ kliṣṭo na yanmudhā |
07862 svarūpānanurūpeṇa candanasya phalena kim ||
07871 mayā badaralubdhena vṛkṣāṇāmanabhijñayā
07872 vane kaṇṭakasādṛśyātkhadiraḥ paryupāsitaḥ ||
07881 mahātarurvā bhavati samūlo vā vinaśyati |
07882 nāṅkuraprakriyāmeti nyagrodhakaṇikāṅkuraḥ ||
07891 puṣpapattraphalacchāyāmūlavalkaladārubhiḥ |
07892 dhanyā mahīruhā yeṣāṃ vimukhā yānti nārthinaḥ ||
07901 patatyaṅgāravarṣe vā vāti vā pralayānile |
07902 tālaḥ stabdhatayārabdhastayaiva saha naśyati ||
07911 chāyāvanto gatavyālāḥ svārohāḥ phaladāyinaḥ |
07912 mārgadrumā mahāntaśca pareṣāmeva bhūtaye ||
07921 agatīnāṃ khalīkārādduḥkhaṃ naivopajāyate |
07922 bhavantyaśokāḥ prāyeṇa sāṅkurāḥ pādatāḍitāḥ ||
07931 yadyapi candanaviṭapī vidhinā phalakusumavarjito vihitaḥ |
07932 nijavapuṣaiva pareṣāṃ tathāpi saṃtāpamapaharati ||
07941 prāpte vasantamāse vṛddhiṃ prāpnoti sakalavanarājinaḥ |
07942 yanna karīre pattraṃ tatkiṃ doṣo vasantasya ||
07951 phalitaghanaviṭapavighaṭitapaṭudinakaramahasi lasati kalpatarau |

07961 phalakusumakisalayojjvalaviṭapaśatāntaritataraṇikiraṇaughe |
07962 mārgatarau nikaṭasthe kaḥ pathikaḥ klāntimanubhavati ||
07971 dūrīkṛtasvārthalavā janasya
07972 samudyatā ye bhuvi tāpaśāntyai |
07973 drumāsta evāgatikā na vidmaḥ
07974 prajāpaterāśayaleśamatra ||
07981 candane viṣadharānsahāmahe
07982 vastu sundaramaguptimatkutaḥ |
07983 rakṣituṃ vada kimātmasauṣṭhavaṃ
07984 saṃcitāḥ khadira kaṇṭakāstvayā ||
07991 grathita eṣa mithaḥ kṛtaśṛṅkhalo
07992 viṣadharairadhiruhya mahājaḍaḥ |
07993 malayajaḥ sumanobhiranāśrito
07994 yadata eva phalena na yujyate ||
08001 yatkiṃcanānucitamapyucitānubandhi
08002 kiṃ candanasya na kṛtaṃ kusumaṃ phalaṃ vā |
08003 lajjāmahe bhṛśamapakrama eva yātus
08004 tasyāntikaṃ parigṛhītabṛhatkuṭhāraḥ ||
08011 he bālacampakataro taruṇīkapola
08012 lāvaṇyacumbanasukhocitacārupuṣpa |
08013 kiṃ puṣpitena vijahīha vikāsahāsa
08014 muddāmapāmaragaṇā marubhūmireṣā ||
08021 antaḥprataptamarusaikatadahyamāna
08022 mūlasya campakataroḥ kva vikāsacintā |
08023 prāyo bhavatyanucitasthitideśabhājāṃ
08024 śreyaḥ svajīvaparipālanamātrameva ||
08031 daurjanyamātmani paraṃ prathitaṃ vidhātrā
08032 bhūrjadrumasya viphalatvasamarpaṇena |
08033 kiṃ carmabhirniśitaśastraśatāvakṛttair
08034 āśāṃ na pūrayati sorthiparamparāṇām ||
08035 kiṃ kaṇṭakaikarasikena phaladviṣā kiṃ
08036 vairasyasīmani kimu sthirakautukena |
08037 chāyāvilāsavimukhena satāṃ kimaṅga
08038 chātrā khalena khadiradruma eṣa sṛṣṭaḥ ||
08051 labdhaṃ cirādamṛtavatkimamṛtyave syād

08053 etatphalaṃ yadayamadhvagaśāpadagdhaḥ
08054 stabdhaḥ phalaṃ phalati varṣaśatena tālaḥ ||
08061 he vṛkṣa śobhita mahāphalabhāralakṣmyā
08062 kṣuttāpaśāntijanakaikajagatprasiddha |
08063 tvatto mayā kathamapīdamadho nirastam
08064 ekaṃ phalaṃ śakunikhaṇḍitamalpamāptam ||
08071 atyantaśītalatayā subhagasvabhāva
08072 satyaṃ na kaścidapi te tarurasti tulyaḥ |
08073 chāyārthināmapi punarvikaṭadvijihva
08074 saṅgena candana viṣadrumanirviśeṣaḥ ||
08081 kathamiyati vanānte kaścideko na tādṛg
08082 varavanataruruccaiḥ puṣpavallīphalāḍhyaḥ |
08083 jagadasukhavidhāturdagdhadhāturniyogād
08084 dhavakhadirapalāśāḥ kevalaṃ vṛddhibhājaḥ ||
08091 śākhāsaṃtatisaṃniruddhagaganābhogasya labdhvā taroś
08092 chāyāṃ yasya bhavidbhireva śamitā gharmāpadonekaśaḥ |
08093 bhoḥ pānthā nanu dṛśyatāṃ vidhigatistasyaiva kālakṣaya
08094 prakṣīṇasya taledya taptasikatāṅgāraivaḥ paraṃ dahyate ||
08101 citrairyasya patittribhirdaśadiśo bhrāntvā sametaiḥ sukhaṃ
08102 viśrāntaṃ śayitaṃ prabhuktamuṣitaṃ skandhe phalaiḥ praśrite |
08103 tasyaivonmathitasya duṣṭakariṇā mārgadrumasyādhunā
08104 kārīṣāya kaṣanti śoṣaparuṣāṃ gopālabālāstvacam ||
08111 saṃtoṣaḥ kimaśaktatā kimathavā tasminnasaṃbhāvanā
08112 śobhaivātha ca kānanisthitiriyaṃ pradveṣa evāthavā |
08113 āstāṃ khalvanurūpayā saphalayā puṣpaśriyā durvidhe
08114 saṃbandhonanurūpayāpi na kṛtaḥ kiṃ candanasya tvayā ||
08121 sanmūlaḥ prathinnatirghanalasacchāyaḥ sthitaḥ satpathe
08122 sevyaḥ sadbhiritīdamākalayatā tālodhvagenāśritaḥ |
08123 puṃsaḥ śaktiriyatyasau sa tu phaledadyāthavā śvothavā
08124 kāle kvāpyathavā kadācidathavā netyatra vedhāḥ prabhuḥ ||
08131 yajjātosi catuṣpathe ghanalasacchāyosi kiṃ chāyayā
08132 saṃyuktaḥ phalitosi kiṃ yadi phalaiḥ pūrṇosi kiṃ saṃnataḥ |
08133 he sadvṛkṣa sahasva saṃprati sakhe śākhāśikhākarṣaṇa
08134 kṣobhāmoṭanabhañjanāni janataḥ svaireva duścoṣṭitaiḥ ||
08141 suskandhasya visārisaurabhaguṇākrāntākhilāśasya te
08142 tanvīcārupayodharāntarakṛtasparśasya gopyākṛteḥ |
08143 doṣaḥ kopi bhujaṃgasaṃgamakṛtaḥ prodgūta eṣodhunā
08144 yena tvāṃ parihṛtya candanataro yāntyadhvagā dūrataḥ ||
08151 chinnastaptasuhṛtsa candanataruryūyaṃ palāyyāgatā
08152 bhogābhyāsasukhāsikāḥ pratidinaṃ tā vismṛtāstatra vaḥ |
08153 daṃṣṭrākoṭiviṣolkayā pratikṛtaṃ tasya praharturna cet
08154 kiṃ tenaiva saha svayaṃ na nidhanaṃ yātāḥstha bho bhoginaḥ ||
08161 tvanmūle puruṣāyuṣaṃ gatamidaṃ kālena saṃśaṣyatāṃ
08162 ksodīyāṃsamapi kṣaṇaṃ paramataḥ śaktiḥ kutaḥ prāṇitum |
08163 tatsvastyastu vivṛddhimehi mahatīmadyāpi kā nastvarā
08164 kalyāṇaiḥ phalitāsi tālaviṭapinputreṣu pautreṣu vā ||
08171 chāyāsyaiva ghanāsugandhirayamevāpannatāpacchidā
08172 magresyaiva guṇagrahaḥ saguṇatā kiṃ candanasyocyatām |
08173 ā mūlātpunareṣu baddhavṛtibhirvyālaistathā dūṣito
08174 jāne yena varaṃ dhavotha khadiropyanyothavā na tvayam ||
08181 na ślāṣyāni phalāni pallavakṛtā chāyā na vāñchāpi sā
08182 no puṣpaṃ sumanoharaṃ na vihagāḥ śabdāmṛtasyandinaḥ |
08183 kākavrātapurīṣanirbharajaranmūrteraśuddhātmano
08184 niḥstabdhasya taroradhaḥ kathamaho sṛṣṭosi durvedhasā ||
08191 nāsya svāduphalaṃ na cāru kusumaṃ na snigdhaparṇā latā
08192 na cchāyā klamahāriṇī na ca kalakvāṇāstathā patriṇaḥ |
08193 eṣosau khadiradumaḥ kimathavā pānthena dṛṣṭastvayā
08194 tatkiṃ pāntha kaṭhorakaṇṭakamukhairgātrakṣatārthī bhavān ||
08201 snigdhāḥ pallavinaḥ prakāmaviṭapavyāviddhacaṇḍātapā
08202 namrāḥ svāduphalā samāśritajanakṣuttāpavicchedinaḥ |
08203 dagdhāste taravaḥ prayāntu pathikāsteṣveva mārgeṣvamī
08204 rūkṣāḥ kāṇṭakinaḥ sasarpavivarā bhūyaḥ prarūḍhā drumāḥ ||
08211 chāyā nātmana eva yā kathamasāvanyasya niṣpragrahā
08212 grīṣmoṣmāpadi śītalastalabhūvi spandonilādeḥ kutaḥ |
08213 vārtā varṣaśate gate kila phalaṃ bhāvīti vārtaiva sā
08214 drāghimṇā muṣitāḥ kiyacciramaho tālena bālā vayam ||
08221 kastvaṃ bhoḥ kathayāmi daivahatakaṃ māṃ viddhi śāhoṭakaṃ
08222 vairāgyādiva vakṣi sādhu viditaṃ kasmādidaṃ kathyate |
08223 vāmenātra vaṭastamadhvagajanaḥ sarvātmanā sevate
08224 na cchāyāpi paropakārakṛtaye mārgasthitasyāpi se ||
08231 āmodairmaruto mṛgāḥ kisalayairlambaistvacā tāpasāḥ
08232 puṣpaiḥ ṣaṭcaraṇāḥ phalaiḥ śakunayo gharmārditāśchāyayā |
08233 skandhairgandhagajāśca viśramarujāḥ śaśvadvibhaktāstvayā
08234 prāptastvaṃ druma bodhisattvapadavīṃ satyaṃ kujātāḥ pare ||
08241 bhrāmyadbhṛṅgabharāvanamrakusumacyotanmadhūdgandhiṣu
08242 cchāyāvatsu taleṣu pānthanivahā viśramya geheṣviva |
08243 nityaṃ nirjharavārivāritatṛṣastṛpyanti yeṣāṃ phalais
08244 te nandantu phalantu yāntu ca parāmatyunnatiṃ pādapāḥ ||
08251 he he maṇḍitamārga mārgaviṭapiñjīvyāḥ samāḥ śāśvatīr
08252 adyāpyāvṛṇu diktaṭāni viṭapaiḥ sālaiścucumbāmbaram |
08253 mūle viśramaṇāśayaiva luṭhitā yanna tvayā kevalaṃ
08254 gharmārteḥ parimocitāḥ phalaśatairyāvadvayaṃ tarpitāḥ ||
08261 vṛddhiryasya tarormanorathaśatairāśāvatā prārthitā
08262 jātosau sarasaḥ pravāsiphaladaḥ sarvāśritāpāśrayaḥ |
08263 nānādeśasamāgatairaviditairākrāntamanyaiḥ khagais
08264 taṃ labdhāvasaropi vṛddhaśakunirdūre sthito vīkṣate ||
08271 dāvāgniploṣaduḥkhaṃ kharapavanajalakleśamarkācca tāpaṃ
08272 mātaṅgākarṣaṇāni vyasanamapi guru prāptavantopi vajrāt |
08273 dārucchāyāphalāni tvacamapi kusumaṃ mañjarīḥ pallavānvā
08274 nārthibhyo vārayanti pratidivasamaho sādhu vṛttaṃ tarūṇām ||
08281 haṃsāḥ padmavanāśayā balibhujo gṛdhrāśca māṃsāśayā
08282 pānthāḥ svāduphalāśayā madhulihaḥ saurabhyagandhāśayā |
08283 dūrānniṣphalaraktapuṣpanicayairniḥsāra rathyonnate
08284 re  re śalmalipādapa pratidinaṃ ke na tvayā vañcitāḥ ||
08291 bhrātarbhīmamarubhramśramaśamavyāpārapāraṃgamaṃ
08292 matvā candanapādapaṃ pathika mā viśrāntaye śiśriyaḥ |
08293 etasyāntikavartibhirghanaviṣajvālāvalībīṣaṇair
08294 āśvāsya smṛtiśeṣatāṃ viṣadharairnīṃtaḥ kiyantodhvagāḥ ||
08301 uccairyo madhupānalubdhamanasāṃ bhṛṅgāṅganānāṃ gaṇair
08302 udgīto racitālayaḥ khagakulairdeśāntarādāgataiḥ |
08303 āsīdyaśva niṣevitodhvagaśatairgrīṣmoṣmatānticchide
08304 soyaṃ saṃprati durmadena dalitaśchāyātarurdantinā ||
08311 bhuktaṃ svāduphalaṃ kṛtaṃ ca śayanaṃ śākhāgrajaiḥ pallavais
08312 tvacchāyāpariśītalaṃ ca salilaṃ pītaṃ vinītaḥ klamaḥ |
08313 viśrāntaṃ suciraṃ tatopi manasā prāptā parā nirvṛtis
08314 tvaṃ sanmārgatarurvayaṃ ca pathikā bhūyāspunaḥ saṃgamaḥ ||
08321 astyeva bhūbhṛtāṃ mūrdhni divi vā dyotatembudaḥ |
08322 marudbhirbhujyamānopi sa kimeti rasātalam ||
08331 ambudaḥ kṛtapado nabhastale
08332 toyapūraparipūritodadhiḥ |
08333 goṣpadasya bharaṇepyaśaktimān
08334 ityasatyamabhidīyate katham ||
08341 etadatra pathikaikajīvitaṃ
08342 paśya śuṣyati kathaṃ mahatsaraḥ |
08343 dhiṅmudhāmbudhara ruddhasadgatir
08344 vardhitā kimiha haṭṭavāhinī ||
08351 svārthānapekṣaṃ janatāpaśāntyai
08352 nityoditāḥ santi payomucomī |
08353 vivarṣiṇastānavagṛhṇate ye
08354 santyeva te kepi mahānubhāvāḥ ||
08361 kva dṛṣṭamandhena balāhakena
08362 ghrātuṃ gavā yanna tṛṇaṃ nighṛṣṭam |
08363 mahātarurbandhurivādhvagānā
08364 māyātvavaśyāyakaṇairdaridraḥ ||
08371 uttuṅgaśailaśikharāśrayaṇena kecid
08372 uddāmavīcivalitāḥ sarito bhavanti |
08373 anye punarjalakaṇāstṛṇaloṣṭapātā
08374 dambhomucāṃ payasi na kṣayamānpuvanti ||
08381 yatroṣitosi cirakālamakiṃcanaḥ san
08382 narṇaḥpratigrahadhanagrahaṇādhamarṇaḥ |
08383 nirlajja garjasi samudrataṭepi tatra
08384 dhṛṣṭodhamastava samo ghana naiva dṛṣṭaḥ ||
08391 āsyaṃ nirasya rasitaiḥ suciraṃ vihasya
08392 gātrāntareṣu ghana varṣasi cātakasya |
08393 taccañcukoṭikuṭilāyatakaṃdharasya
08394 prāṇātyayosya bhavataḥ parihāsamātram ||
08401 ākramyoccaiḥ śirasi vasatirbhūbhṛtāmunnatānāṃ
08402 toyādānāṃ tadapi jaladherlokasaṃtāpaśāntyai |
08403 dīrghā chāyā prakṛtimahati vyomni cābhogabandho
08404 he he megha spṛhayati na te kaḥ kiletthaṃ vratāya ||
08411 ākramyoccaiḥ śirasi vasatirbhūbhṛtāmunnatānāṃ
08412 toyādānaṃ tadapi jaladherlokasaṃtāpaśāntyai |
08413 dīrghā chāyā prakṛtimahati vyomni cābhogabandho
08414 he he megha spṛhayati na te kaḥ kiletthaṃ vratāya ||
08421 sādhūtpātaghanaugha sādhu sudhiyāṃ dheyaṃ dharāyāmidaṃ
08422 konyaḥ kartumalaṃ tavaiva ghaṭate karmedṛśaṃ duṣkaram |
08423 sarvasyaupayikāni yāni katicitkṣetrāṇi tatrāśaniḥ
08424 sarvānaupayikeṣu dagdhasikatāraṇyeṣvapāṃ vṛṣṭayaḥ ||
08431 bhekaiḥ koṭaraśāyibhirmṛtamiva kṣmāntargataṃ kacchapaiḥ
08432 pāṭhīnaiḥ pṛthupaṅkakūṭaluṭhitairyasminmuhurmūrchitam |
08433 tasmiñchuṣkasarasyakālajaladenāgatya tacceṣṭitaṃ
08434 yenā kaṇṭhanimagnavanyakariṇāṃ yūthaiḥ payaḥ pīyate ||
08441 yadbhūbhṛto laghuguṇairapi baddhamūlam
08442 āpāditāni sahasaiva tṛṇaiḥ śirāṃsi |
08443 ambhomucaḥ pracuravarṣaviśṛṅkhalasya
08444 tacceṣṭitaṃ duravadhāragaterjalasya ||
08451 kṛchrāddatte viralaviralānvāribindūnpravṛddho
08452 garjatyekaḥ sarabhasataraṃ paśya tanmātralābhāt |
08453 nṛtyatyanyopyatulamahimaślāghyabhūmirna jāne
08454 madhyādābhyāṃ vipulahṛdayaścātakaḥ kiṃ nu meghaḥ ||
08461 gatāste jīmūtāḥ sphuradalikulaśyāmavapuṣaḥ
08462 śriyā yeṣāṃ loke sthalajalavibhāgopyapahṛtaḥ |
08463 vṛthā tṛṣṇāndhaḥ kiṃ bhramasi vidhuraścātakaśiśo
08464 śarajjīmūtoyaṃ kuta iha payobindurapi te ||
08471 paśyāmaḥ kimayaṃ viceṣṭata iti svalpābhrasiddhikriyair
08472 darpāddūramupekṣitena balavatkarmeritairmantribhiḥ |
08473 labdhātmaprasareṇa rakṣitumathāśakyena muktvāśaniṃ
08474 sphītastāvadaho ghanena ripuṇā dagdho girigrāmakaḥ ||
08481 garjitvā bahu saṃnirudhya gaganaṃ pracchādya diṅmaṇḍalaṃ
08482 saṃpādyoddalitendranīlaśakalaśyāmābhirāmaṃ vapuḥ |
08483 prāpte vāridharāgamepi salilaṃ tattyaktamambhomucā
08484 cañcūśrātakapotakasya sakalā siktā na yena svayam ||
08491 atrotpātaghanena mantrivikale śūnyāmbaravyāpinā
08492 dhṛṣṭasvaprakṛtikriyāsamucite grāme tathā jṛmbhitam |
08493 rathyākardamavāhināmatiśucisvacchātmanāmantaraṃ
08494 nāpyajñāyi janairyathaughapayasāṃ strotojalānāmapi ||
08501 re meghāḥ svaśarīradānaguru kiṃ bauddhaṃ yaśo na śrutaṃ
08502 yaṣmābhiḥ kimu pārijātacaritaṃ nākarṇitaṃ vā kvacit |
08503 yenaitatsukhalabhyamambu dadatāṃ yuṣmākamudgarjatāṃ
08504 no lajjāpyabhijāyatetirabhasādvyomnyuddhataṃ dhāvatām ||
08511 sthāne varṣati naiva garjati vṛthā klāntiṃ haratyañjasā
08512 kṣetrāṇāṃ paritāpajarjararucāṃ kṣemaṃkaraḥ kṣmātale |
08513 yadyadbhadrakasāndratāṃ hṛdi dadhātyanyatkarotyullasa
08514 nsanmeghoyamamoghadarśanaghanasnigdhacchavirvardhatām ||
08521 no garjatyamburāśistrijagadadhipatiprārthitārthapradāna
08522 vyāpārsphītakīrtiḥ sphuradanlaśikhānargharatnaikapūrṇaḥ |
08523 tattoyastokamātravyapahṛtavikṛtiḥ prākṛtoyaṃ prakṛtyā
08524 śūnye kṣiptvāmbu garjatyagaṇitanidhano vārivāhaḥ sagarvam ||
08531 na pālayati maryādāṃ velākhyāmambudhistathā |
08532 tṛṣyatāṃ nopakartavyamitīmāmaparāṃ yathā ||
08541 gavādīnāṃ payonyedyuḥ sadyo vā dadhi jāyate |
08542 kṣīrodadhestu nādyāpi mahatāṃ vikṛtiḥ kutaḥ ||
08551 yadyapi svacchabhāvena darśayatyudadhirmaṇīn |
08552 tathāpi jānudaghnoyamiti cetasi mā kṛthāḥ ||
08561 yasyāmbukaṇikāpyāsye na patatyarthīnāṃ kvacit |
08562 kaṣṭamambhonidhiḥ sopi nadīna iti kathyate ||
08571 yātu nāśaṃ samudrasya mahimā sa bhuvi śrutaḥ |
08572 vāḍavaḥ kṣutpipāsārto yenaikopi na tarpitaḥ ||
08581 amṛtarasavisaraavitaraṇamaraṇottāritasure sati payodhau |
08582 kasya sphuranti hṛdaye grīṣmataḍākā bhuvi varākāḥ ||
08591 yadayaṃ śaśiśekharo haro
08592 harirapyeṣa yadīśitā śriyaḥ |
08593 amarā api yatsurā amī
08594 tadimāstasya vibhūtivipruṣaḥ ||
08601 kṣārataiva hi guṇastathāsti te
08602 yena na vrajati kaścidantikam |
08603 bhīṣaṇākṛti bibharṣi yādasāṃ
08604 cakramarṇava kimarthamagrataḥ ||

10143 aṅgulyagralaghukriyāpravilayinyādīyamāne śanais
10144 tatroḍḍīya gate hahetyanudinaṃ nidrāti nāntaḥśucā ||
10151 āstetraiva sarasyaho bata kiyānsaṃtoṣapakṣagraho
10152 haṃsasyāsya manāṅna dhāvati matiḥ śrīdhāmni padme kvacit |
10153 suptodyāpi vibudhyate na taditastāvatpratīkṣāmahe
10154 velāmityuṣasi priyā madhulihaḥ soḍhuṃ tu eva kṣamāḥ ||
10161 vātāhāratayā jagadviṣadharairāśvāsya niḥśeṣitaṃ
10162 te grastāḥ punarabhratoyakaṇikātīvravratairbarhibhiḥ |
10163 tepi krūracamūrucarmavasanairnītāḥ kṣayaṃ lubdhakair
10164 dambhasya sphuritaṃ vidannapi jano jālmo guṇānīhate ||
10171 nāmāpyanyatarornimīlitamabhūttattāvadunmīlitaṃ
10172 prasthāne skhalataḥ svavartmani vidheranyairgṛhītaḥ karaḥ |
10173 lokaścāyamadṛṣṭadarśanadaśādṛgvaiśasāduddhṛto
10174 yuktaṃ kāṣṭhika lūnavānyadasitāmāmrālimākālikīm ||
10181 ūḍhā yena mahādhuraḥ suviṣame mārge sadaikākinā
10182 soḍho yena kadācideva na nije goṣṭhenyaśauṇḍadhvaniḥ |
10183 āsīdyaśca gavāṃ gaṇasya tilakastasyaiva saṃpratyaho
10184 dhikkaṣṭaṃ dhavalasya jātajaraso goḥ paṇyamuddhoṣyate ||
10191 bhekena kvaṇatā saroṣaparuṣaṃ yatkṛṣṇasarpānane
10192 dātuṃ karṇacapeṭamujjhitabhiyā hastaḥ samullāsitaḥ |
10193 yaccādhomukhamakṣiṇī pidadhatā nāgena tatra sthitaṃ
10194 tatsarvaṃ viṣamantriṇo bhagavataḥ kasyāpi līlāyitam ||
10201 nityaṃ tīrthe nivāsaḥ prakṛtiratitarāṃ snigdhamugdhasvabhāvā
10202 vṛttirdaivāddhi vaktre gagananipatitairnirmalairvārileśaiḥ |
10203 itthaṃ sarvaṃ vilokya prakaṭamiha time mugdhalokena loke
10204 sādhutvaṃ darśitaṃ te bahirabahiramī kaṇṭakāḥ kena dṛṣṭāḥ ||
10211 mṛtyorāsyamivātataṃ dhanuramī cāśīviṣābhāḥ śarāḥ
10212 śikṣā sāpi jitārjunā pratibhayaṃ sarvāṅganimnā gatiḥ |
10213 antaḥ krauryamaho śaṭhasya madhuraṃ hā hāri geyaṃ mukhe
10214 vyādhasyāsya yathā bhaviṣyati tathā manye vanaṃ nirmṛgam ||
10221 dhigvyomno mahimānametu dalaśaḥ proccaistadīyaṃ padaṃ
10222 nindyāṃ daivagatiṃ prayātvabhavanistasyāstu śūnyasya vā |
10223 yenotkṣiptakarasya naṣṭamahasaḥ śrāntasya saṃtāpino
10224 mitrasyāpi nirāśrayasya na kṛtaṃ dhṛtyai karālambanam ||
10231 digdāhaikarate vanāntakara te jvālā na me rocate
10232 dugdhaṃ svāśrayamudyatasya bhavato necchanti vṛddhiṃ janāḥ |
10233 mūlānyasya mahībhṛto dalayituṃ durvedhasā nirmitaḥ
10234 ko vā na tvayi śaṅkate khala jagatkhedāvahe dāva he ||
10241 kṣutkṣāmeṇa kathaṃ kathaṃcidaniśaṃ gātraṃ kṛśaṃ bibhratā
10242 bhrāntaṃ yena gṛhe gṛhe gṛhavatāmucchiṣṭapiṇḍārthinā |
10243 asthnaḥ khaṇḍamavāpya daivapatitaṃ śūnyāṃ trilokīmimāṃ
10244 manvāno dhigaho sa eva saramāputrodya siṃhāyate ||
10251 śuṣkasnāyuvasāvasekamalinaṃ nirmāṃsamapyasthi goḥ
10252 śvā labdhā paritoṣameti na tu tattasya kṣudhaḥ śāntaye |
10253 siṃho jambukamaṅkamāgatamapi tyaktvā nihanti dvipaṃ
10254 sarvaḥ kṛchragatopi vāñchati janaḥ sattvānurūpaṃ phalam ||
10261 kāluṣyaṃ payasāṃ vilokya śanakairuḍḍīya haṃsā gatā
10262 dhārājarjarakesarāsphuṭarucaḥ padmā nimignā jale |
10263 sā sarvartusukhāvatārapadavī channā tṛṇairnūtanaiḥ
10264 kaṣṭaṃ tādṛgapi svabhāvavimalaṃ vṛdchyaiva naṣṭaṃ saraḥ ||
10271 ye saṃtoṣasukhaprabuddhamanasasteṣāmabhinno mṛdo
10272 yepyete dhanalobhasaṃkuladhiyasteṣāṃ tu dūre nṛṇām |
10273 itthaṃ kasya kṛte kṛtaḥ sa vidhinā tādṛkpadaṃ saṃpadāṃ
10274 svātmanyeva samāptahemamahimā merurna me rocate ||
10281 dravyāṇāmadharottaravyatikaro bhagnāśayānāmadho
10282 bījānāṃ nayanaṃ svayaṃ ca nijavacchidrakriyānveṣaṇam |
10283 vyūhābandhavidhāyibhirgatabhayairmugdhaprasuptārbhakaṃ
10284 śūnyaṃ prāpya nivāsamākhubhiraho kiṃ kiṃ na yadyatkṛtam ||
10291 anyonyasya layaṃ bhayādiva mahābhūteṣu yāteṣvalaṃ
10292 kalpānte parameka eva sa taruḥ skandhoccayairjṛmbhate |
10293 vinyasya trijaganti kukṣikuhare devena yasyāsyate
10294 śākhāgre śiśuneva sevitajalakrīḍāvilāsālasam ||trivikramasya
10301 trailokyopakṛtiprasaktamanaso devasya śaṃbhoḥ priyā
10302 jātā śailakule varairabhimatairānandayantī surān |
10303 mlecchānāmapi vāñchitārpaṇaparā svasyāspadasyāmbikā
10304 vindhyasyonnatimātanoti na nijāṃ daivasya kīdṛgbalam ||bhā. amṛtadattasya
10311 kiṃ tvaṃ hālika mūḍhadhīrhataphalaṃ mā mā kṛthā lāṅgalaṃ
10312 kṣetraṃ naiva bhavatyadhaḥ kaṭhinatā naivātra dṛṣṭā tvayā |
10313 ullekhopi na jāyatetra virama kleśaḥ phalaṃ kevalaṃ
10314 nirbījā bahavo gatāśca satataṃ dṛṣṭāḥ śrutā vā na kim ||kasyāpi
10321 koyaṃ bhrāntiprakārastava pavana ghanāvaskarasthānajātaṃ
10322 tejasvivrātasevye nabhasi nayasi yatpāṃsupūraṃ pratiṣṭhām |
10323 yasminnutthāpyamāne jananayanapathopadravastāvadāstāṃ
10324 kenopāyena sahyo vapuṣi malinatādoṣa eṣa tvayaiva ||bhā. amṛtadattasya
10331 jātaḥ kūrmaḥ sa ekaḥ pṛthubhuvanabharāyārpitaṃ yena pṛṣṭhaṃ
10332 ślāghyaṃ janma dhruvasya bhramati niyamitaṃ yatra tejasvicakram |
10333 saṃjātavyarthapakṣāḥ parahitakaraṇe nopariṣṭānna cādho
10334 brahmāṇḍodumbarāntarmaśakavadapare jantavo jātanaṣṭāḥ ||kasyāpi
10341 kaṭu raṭasi kimevaṃ karṇayoḥ kuñjarāre
10342 raviditanijabuddhe kiṃ na vijñātamasti |
10343 śiratarakaradaṃṣṭrāṭaṅkanirbhinnakumbhaṃ
10344 maśaka galakarandhre hastiyūthaṃ mamajja ||kasyāpi
10351 udrarjankuṭilastaṭāśrayatarupronmūlanoḍḍāmaro
10352 mā garvīḥ saritaḥ pravāha jaladhiṃ prakṣobhayāmīti bhoḥ |
10353 svāṃ sattāṃ yadi vāñchasi bhrama maruṣvevāḥsva tatraiva vā
10354 dūre vāḍavavahniratra tu mahāsattvairviśanpīyase ||
10361 sthairyaṃ tuṅgaśirā jagatsthitikṛte velāmahībhṛcchrito
10362 dūrātpreraṇayā kalāvata imaṃ krāntuṃ jalānāṃ pate |
10363 mithyā vāñchasi kiṃ tatastava paraṃ syādratnasattvakṣayo
10364 nūnaṃ ghaṭṭanamāpya pādatalagastvasyaiva cānte luṭheḥ ||
10371 āndolayasyavirataṃ gaganārkamaṅke
10372 tārāgaṇaṃ ca śaśinaṃ ca tathetarāṇi |
10373 tejāṃsi bhāsurataḍitprabhṛtīni sādho
10374 citraṃ tathāpi na jahāsi yadāndhyamantaḥ ||
10381 ayaṃ sa bhuvanatrayaprathitasaṃyamaḥ śaṃkaro
10382 bibharti vapuṣādhunā virahakātaraḥ kāminīm |
10383 anena kila nirjitā vayamiti priyāyāḥ karaṃ
10384 kareṇa paripīḍayañjayati jātahāsaḥ smaraḥ ||
10391 bhrūśārṅgākṛṣṭamuktāḥ kuvalayamadhupavyomalakṣmīmuṣo ye
10392 kṣīvā ye kṛṣṇaśārā narahṛdayabhidastārakakrūraśalyāḥ |
10393 te dīrghāpāṅgapuṅkhāḥ smitaviṣaviṣamāḥ pakṣmalāḥ strīkaṭākṣāḥ
10394 pāyāsurvotivīryāstribhuvanajayinaḥ pañcabāṇasya bāṇāḥ ||
10401 gaccha gacchasi cetkānta panthānaḥ santu te śivāḥ |
10402 mamāpi janma tatraiva bhūyādyatra gato bhavān ||
